    <script>
      (function() {
        var lastAddedProductTime = 0;
        function wrapTrekkieTrack() {
          if (window.ShopifyAnalytics && window.ShopifyAnalytics.lib && typeof window.ShopifyAnalytics.lib.track === 'function') {
            if (window.ShopifyAnalytics.lib.track._dedupeWrapped) return;
            var origTrack = window.ShopifyAnalytics.lib.track;
            
            var wrappedTrack = function(eventName, properties, options, callback, metadata) {
              var eName = String(eventName || '').toLowerCase();
              if (eName.indexOf('added product') !== -1 || eName.indexOf('added_to_cart') !== -1 || eName.indexOf('addtocart') !== -1) {
                var now = Date.now();
                if (now - lastAddedProductTime < 2000) {
                  if (typeof callback === 'function') callback();
                  return;
                }
                lastAddedProductTime = now;
              }
              return origTrack.apply(this, arguments);
            };
            
            wrappedTrack._dedupeWrapped = true;
            window.ShopifyAnalytics.lib.track = wrappedTrack;
          }
        }
        wrapTrekkieTrack();
        setInterval(wrapTrekkieTrack, 250);
      })();
    </script>

<script>
  function getOrCreateDeviceId() {
    var deviceId = '';
    try {
      deviceId = localStorage.getItem('fit11_device_id') || localStorage.getItem('wa_device_id') || localStorage.getItem('__kp_device_id') || '';
      if (!deviceId) {
        deviceId = 'dev_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9) + '_' + Date.now();
        localStorage.setItem('fit11_device_id', deviceId);
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    return deviceId;
  }

  async function autoFetchPhoneByDeviceId() {
    try {
      var device_id = getOrCreateDeviceId();
      var cachedPhone = localStorage.getItem('fit11_verified_phone') || localStorage.getItem('kwikpass_user_phone') || sessionStorage.getItem('fit11_verified_phone');
      
      if (!device_id && !cachedPhone) return null;
      
      var bodyParams = { merchant_key: 'sk_live_11fit_106b31bb8dd7a7' };
      if (device_id) bodyParams.device_id = device_id;
      if (cachedPhone) bodyParams.phone = cachedPhone;

      var res = await fetch('https://checkout-app-one-lilac.vercel.app/api/identify', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(bodyParams)
      });
      var data = await res.json();
      if (data && data.identified) {
        if (data.first_name) {
          localStorage.setItem('fit11_first_name', data.first_name);
        }
        // Use masked_phone or cachedPhone as the identifier we return
        var returnedPhone = data.phone || data.masked_phone || cachedPhone;
        if (returnedPhone) {
          localStorage.setItem('fit11_verified_phone', returnedPhone);
          sessionStorage.setItem('fit11_verified_phone', returnedPhone);
        }
        if (data.email) {
          localStorage.setItem('fit11_verified_email', data.email);
        }
        return returnedPhone;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Auto-fetch user identity failed:', e);
    }
    return null;
  }

  async function openTxAccountModal(tab) {
    var overlay = document.getElementById('tx-login-modal-overlay');
    var iframe = document.getElementById('tx-account-iframe');
    if (overlay && iframe) {
      overlay.style.display = 'flex';
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      var device_id = getOrCreateDeviceId();
      var savedPhone = '';
      try {
        savedPhone = localStorage.getItem('fit11_verified_phone') || localStorage.getItem('kwikpass_user_phone') || sessionStorage.getItem('fit11_verified_phone') || '';
      } catch (e) {}

      // Auto-fetch phone number by device ID from Checkout App database if not already cached
      if (!savedPhone) {
        savedPhone = await autoFetchPhoneByDeviceId() || '';
      }

      var portalUrl = 'https://checkout-app-one-lilac.vercel.app/account?merchant_key=sk_live_11fit_106b31bb8dd7a7&store=11fit.in&mode=modal';
      if (device_id) {
        portalUrl += '&device_id=' + encodeURIComponent(device_id);
      }
      if (savedPhone) {
        portalUrl += '&phone=' + encodeURIComponent(savedPhone);
      }
      if (tab && typeof tab === 'string') {
        portalUrl += '&tab=' + encodeURIComponent(tab);
      }
      if (iframe.src === 'about:blank' || !iframe.src || tab) {
        iframe.src = portalUrl;
      }
    }
  }

  function closeTxAccountModal() {
    var overlay = document.getElementById('tx-login-modal-overlay');
    if (overlay) {
      overlay.style.display = 'none';
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
    }
  }

  function handleTxOverlayClick(e) {
    if (e.target && e.target.id === 'tx-login-modal-overlay') {
      closeTxAccountModal();
    }
  }

  window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {
    if (event.data && event.data.type === 'KWIKPASS_LOGIN_SUCCESS' && event.data.phone) {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem('fit11_verified_phone', event.data.phone);
        localStorage.setItem('kwikpass_user_phone', event.data.phone);
        sessionStorage.setItem('fit11_verified_phone', event.data.phone);
        if (event.data.email) {
          localStorage.setItem('fit11_verified_email', event.data.email);
          localStorage.setItem('kwikpass_user_email', event.data.email);
          sessionStorage.setItem('fit11_verified_email', event.data.email);
        }
        var menuBtn = document.getElementById('tx-menu-login-btn');
        if (menuBtn) menuBtn.innerText = 'My Account';
      } catch (e) {}
    } else if (event.data && event.data.type === 'KWIKPASS_LOGOUT') {
      try {
        localStorage.removeItem('fit11_verified_phone');
        localStorage.removeItem('kwikpass_user_phone');
        sessionStorage.removeItem('fit11_verified_phone');
        localStorage.removeItem('fit11_verified_email');
        localStorage.removeItem('kwikpass_user_email');
        sessionStorage.removeItem('fit11_verified_email');
        var menuBtn = document.getElementById('tx-menu-login-btn');
        if (menuBtn) menuBtn.innerText = 'Login / Sign Up';
      } catch (e) {}
    }
  });

  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    autoFetchPhoneByDeviceId().then(function(fetchedPhone) {
      var phone = fetchedPhone || localStorage.getItem('fit11_verified_phone') || localStorage.getItem('kwikpass_user_phone') || sessionStorage.getItem('fit11_verified_phone');
      if (phone) {
        var firstName = localStorage.getItem('fit11_first_name');
        var menuBtn = document.getElementById('tx-menu-login-btn');
        if (menuBtn) menuBtn.innerText = firstName ? 'My Account' : 'My Account';
        
        var greeting = document.getElementById('tx-drawer-greeting');
        if (greeting && firstName && firstName.trim() !== '' && firstName !== 'null') greeting.innerText = 'Hey ' + firstName + '!';
      }
    });
    var iframe = document.getElementById('tx-account-iframe');
    if (iframe && (iframe.src === 'about:blank' || !iframe.src)) {
      var savedPhone = '';
      try {
        savedPhone = localStorage.getItem('fit11_verified_phone') || localStorage.getItem('kwikpass_user_phone') || sessionStorage.getItem('fit11_verified_phone') || '';
      } catch (e) {}
      var device_id = getOrCreateDeviceId();
      var portalUrl = 'https://checkout-app-one-lilac.vercel.app/account?merchant_key=sk_live_11fit_106b31bb8dd7a7&store=11fit.in&mode=modal';
      if (device_id) {
        portalUrl += '&device_id=' + encodeURIComponent(device_id);
      }
      if (savedPhone) {
        portalUrl += '&phone=' + encodeURIComponent(savedPhone);
      }
      iframe.src = portalUrl;
    }
  });

  // Global Interceptor: ANY link to /account, /account/login, or Order Tracking across the entire store
  // will open OUR custom OTP login/account modal instead of Shopify native pages!
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
    var link = e.target.closest('a[href*="/account"], a[href*="order-tracking"], a[href*="/pages/order-tracking"]');
    if (link && !link.hasAttribute('data-no-intercept') && link.getAttribute('target') !== '_blank') {
      e.preventDefault();
      var details = link.closest('details');
      if (details) details.removeAttribute('open');
      var href = link.getAttribute('href') || '';
      var tab = (href.indexOf('orders') !== -1 || href.indexOf('order-tracking') !== -1) ? 'orders' : '';
      if (typeof openTxAccountModal === 'function') {
        openTxAccountModal(tab);
      }
    }
  });

  // Automatically open OTP modal on Order Tracking page
  if (window.location.pathname.indexOf('order-tracking') !== -1) {
    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
      if (typeof openTxAccountModal === 'function') {
        openTxAccountModal('orders');
      }
    });
  }
</script>

<script>
  // Desktop Auto-Open Submenu on Hover (T-Shirts ⌄, Bottoms ⌄, etc.)
  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    var menuItems = document.querySelectorAll('.header__inline-menu > ul > li');
    menuItems.forEach(function(item) {
      var details = item.querySelector('details');
      if (!details) return;
      var summary = details.querySelector('summary');

      item.addEventListener('mouseenter', function() {
        if (window.innerWidth >= 990) {
          document.querySelectorAll('.header__inline-menu details[open]').forEach(function(other) {
            if (other !== details) {
              other.removeAttribute('open');
              var otherSummary = other.querySelector('summary');
              if (otherSummary) otherSummary.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
            }
          });
          details.setAttribute('open', '');
          if (summary) summary.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
        }
      });

      item.addEventListener('mouseleave', function() {
        if (window.innerWidth >= 990) {
          details.removeAttribute('open');
          if (summary) summary.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
        }
      });
    });
  });
</script>

<script>
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const track = document.getElementById('ukTrack-template--29393177444433__11fit_hero_LwyDx3');
    const dots = document.querySelectorAll('#ukDots-template--29393177444433__11fit_hero_LwyDx3 .fit11-dot');
    const wrapper = document.getElementById('ukCarousel-template--29393177444433__11fit_hero_LwyDx3');
    
    // Grabs the speed from the theme editor settings dynamically
    const speed = parseInt(wrapper.getAttribute('data-speed')) || 3000; 
    let slideIndex = 0;
    const totalSlides = dots.length;
    let autoPlayInterval;

    if (totalSlides < 2) return; 

    function updateDots(index) {
      dots.forEach(dot => dot.classList.remove('active'));
      if(dots[index]) dots[index].classList.add('active');
    }

    function scrollToNext() {
      slideIndex = (slideIndex + 1) % totalSlides;
      const slideWidth = track.clientWidth;
      track.scrollTo({ left: slideWidth * slideIndex, behavior: 'smooth' });
      updateDots(slideIndex);
    }

    function startAutoPlay() {
      autoPlayInterval = setInterval(scrollToNext, speed);
    }

    function stopAutoPlay() {
      clearInterval(autoPlayInterval);
    }

    // Manual Swipe Detection
    track.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
      const scrollPos = track.scrollLeft;
      const slideWidth = track.clientWidth;
      const newIndex = Math.round(scrollPos / slideWidth);
      if (newIndex !== slideIndex && newIndex < totalSlides) {
        slideIndex = newIndex;
        updateDots(slideIndex);
      }
    });

    wrapper.addEventListener('touchstart', stopAutoPlay, {passive: true});
    wrapper.addEventListener('touchend', startAutoPlay, {passive: true});
    wrapper.addEventListener('mouseenter', stopAutoPlay);
    wrapper.addEventListener('mouseleave', startAutoPlay);

    startAutoPlay();
  });
</script></div><div id="shopify-section-template--29393177444433__11fit_marquee1_GXAPyT" class="shopify-section"><style data-shopify>

<script>
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const track = document.getElementById('luxeTrack-template--29393177444433__11fit_category_bubbles_wmm7Lw');
    const wrapper = document.getElementById('luxeWrapper-template--29393177444433__11fit_category_bubbles_wmm7Lw');
    if (!track || !wrapper) return;

    const speed = parseInt(wrapper.getAttribute('data-speed')) || 4000;
    const enableAutoplay = wrapper.getAttribute('data-autoplay') === 'true';
    let autoPlayInterval;

    function scrollToNextLuxe() {
      if (track.scrollWidth <= track.clientWidth) return;
      
      const item = track.querySelector('.luxe-item');
      if (!item) return;
      
      const itemWidth = item.offsetWidth + parseInt(window.getComputedStyle(track).gap || 24);
      const maxScroll = track.scrollWidth - track.clientWidth;

      if (track.scrollLeft >= maxScroll - 10) {
        track.scrollTo({ left: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }); 
      } else {
        track.scrollBy({ left: itemWidth, behavior: 'smooth' });
      }
    }

    function playLuxe() {
      if (!enableAutoplay) return;
      clearInterval(autoPlayInterval);
      autoPlayInterval = setInterval(scrollToNextLuxe, speed);
    }

    function pauseLuxe() {
      clearInterval(autoPlayInterval);
    }



    let isDragging = false;
    let startX, startY;

    const items = track.querySelectorAll('.luxe-item');
    items.forEach(item => {
      item.addEventListener('touchstart', (e) => {
        if (enableAutoplay) pauseLuxe();
        isDragging = false;
        startX = e.touches[0].clientX;
        startY = e.touches[0].clientY;
      }, { passive: true });

      item.addEventListener('touchmove', (e) => {
        if (!startX || !startY) return;
        let deltaX = Math.abs(e.touches[0].clientX - startX);
        let deltaY = Math.abs(e.touches[0].clientY - startY);
        if (deltaX > 8 || deltaY > 8) {
          isDragging = true;
        }
      }, { passive: true });

      item.addEventListener('touchend', (e) => {
        if (enableAutoplay) playLuxe();
        
        if (!isDragging) {
          const href = item.getAttribute('href');
          if (href && href !== '#' && href !== '') {
            window.location.href = href;
          }
        }
      });
    });
    
    if (enableAutoplay) playLuxe();
  });
</script></div><div id="shopify-section-template--29393177444433__11fit_3d_deal_LLyATX" class="shopify-section">

1.4-1.4v-18.8c0-.8-.6-1.4-1.4-1.4h-52.5c-.8 0-1.4.6-1.4 1.4v18.8c0 .8.6 1.4 1.4 1.4zm1.4-18.8h49.6v16h-49.6v-16zm-19.4 28h12.3c.8 0 1.4-.6 1.4-1.4v-15.5c0-.8-.6-1.4-1.4-1.4h-12.3c-.8 0-1.4.6-1.4 .7-.3.7-.7s-.3-.7-.7-.7zm-18.1-25.4h-22.6c-.4 0-.7.3-.7.7s.3.7.7.7h22.6c.4 0 .7-.3.7-.7s-.3-.7-.7-.7zm16.3 0h-9.6c-.4 0-.7.3-.7.7s.3.7.7.7h9.6c.4 0 .7-.3.7-.7s-.3-.7-.7-.7zm-39 251.9H273c.4 0 .7-.3.7-.7 0-.4-.3-.7-.7-.7h-36.2c-.4 0-.7.3-.7.7 0 .3.3.7.7.7zm0-25.4h13.6c.4 0 .7-.3.7-.7 0-.4-.3-.7-.7-.7h-13.6c-.4 0-.7.3-.7.7 0 .4.3.7.7.7zm20.5-149c.8 0 1.4-.6 1.4-1.4v-6.5c0-.8-.6-1.4-1.4-1.4s-1.4.6-1.4 1.4v6.5c0 .8.6 1.4 1.4 1.4zm0 96.1c-.8 0-1.4.6-1.4 1.4v6.5c0 .8.6 1.4 1.4 1.4s1.4-.6 1.4-1.4v-6.5c.1-.8-.6-1.4-1.4-1.4zm48-48c0 .8.6 1.4 1.4 1.4h6.5c.8 0 .4-.1l5.8-3.3c.3-.2.5-.6.3-1-.2-.3-.6-.5-1-.3l-5.8 3.3c-.3.2-.5.6-.3 1 .1.3.3.4.6.4zm-85.9 48l-5.8 3.3c-.3.2-.5.6-.3 1 .1.2.4.4.6.4.1 0 .2 0 .4-.1l5.8-3.3c.3-.2.5-.6.3-1s-.6-.5-1-.3zm68.3 18.1c-.2-.3-.6-.5-1-.3-.3.2-.5.6-.3 1l3.4 5.9c.1.2.4.4.6.4.1 0 .2 0 .4-.1.3-.2.5-.6.3-1l-3.4-5.9zm-50.4-84.3c.1.2.4.4.6.4.1 0 .2 0 .4-.1.3-.2.5-.6.3-1L230 214c-.2-.3-.6-.5-1-.3-.3.2-.5.6-.3 1l3.4 5.9zm49.4.3c.1.1.2.1.4.1s.5-.1.6-.4l3.5-6c.2-.3.1-.8-.3-1-.3-.2-.8-.1-1 .3l-3.5 6c-.2.4 0 .8.3 1zm-48.3 83.7c-.3-.2-.8-.1-1 .3l-3.5 6c-.2.3-.1.8.3 1 .1.1.2.1.4.1s.5-.1.6-.4l3.5-6c.1-.4 0-.8-.3-1zm66.4-16.6l5.8 3.4c.1.1.2.1.4.1s.5-.1.6-.4c.2-.3.1-.8-.3-1l-5.8-3.4c-.3-.2-.8-.1-1 .3-.1.4 0 .8.3 1zM215 237.5l-5.8-3.4c-.3-.2-.8-.1-1 .3-.2.3-.1.8.3 1l5.8 0-.2 0-.4.2-.6.1-.2.3-.3.5-.3l23.7-3zm-24.4-27.8c0-3 .3-6 .9-9 .6 3 .9 6 .9 9v27.8l-1.4.2c-.1 0-.2.1-.4.1v-28.1zm1.6 132.6h21.1c.4 0 .7-.3.7-.7 0-.4-.3-.7-.7-.7h-21.1c-.4 0-.7.3-.7.7 0 .4.3.7.7.7z"/></svg>
            
          </a>
          
        </div>
      
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="luxe-dots" id="luxeDots-template--29393177444433__11fit_3d_deal_LLyATX">
    
      <div class="luxe-dot is-active" data-index="0"></div>
    
      <div class="luxe-dot " data-index="1"></div>
    
      <div class="luxe-dot " data-index="2"></div>
    
      <div class="luxe-dot " data-index="3"></div>
    
      <div class="luxe-dot " data-index="4"></div>
    
  </div>

  <div class="luxe-text-area" id="luxeTextArea-template--29393177444433__11fit_3d_deal_LLyATX">
    <h3 class="luxe-title" id="luxeTitle-template--29393177444433__11fit_3d_deal_LLyATX">Loading...</h3>
    
    <div class="luxe-price-row">
      <span id="luxePrice-template--29393177444433__11fit_3d_deal_LLyATX"><span class="luxe-locked-text">••••••</span></span>
      <span class="luxe-compare" id="luxeCompare-template--29393177444433__11fit_3d_deal_LLyATX"></span>
    </div>

    <a href="#" class="luxe-btn is-unlocked" id="luxeBtn-template--29393177444433__11fit_3d_deal_LLyATX">
      <span class="luxe-btn-progress" style="display:none;"></span>
      <span class="luxe-btn-text">View Deal</span>
    </a>
  </div>

</div>

<script>
(function() {
  function initLuxeDeal(wrapper) {
    if (wrapper.dataset.initialized === 'true') return;
    wrapper.dataset.initialized = 'true';

    const sectionId = wrapper.id.replace('luxe-wrapper-', '');
    const timerHours = parseInt(wrapper.getAttribute('data-timer')) || 12;
    const hrEl = document.getElementById('luxeHr-' + sectionId);
    const minEl = document.getElementById('luxeMin-' + sectionId);
    const secEl = document.getElementById('luxeSec-' + sectionId);
    
    let endTime;
    try {
      endTime = localStorage.getItem('luxe_timer_' + sectionId);
      if (!endTime || new Date().getTime() > endTime) {
        endTime = new Date().getTime() + (timerHours * 60 * 60 * 1000);
        localStorage.setItem('luxe_timer_' + sectionId, endTime);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      endTime = new Date().getTime() + (timerHours * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    }

    function updateTimer() {
      let now = new Date().getTime();
      let distance = endTime - now;
      if (distance < 0) {
        endTime = new Date().getTime() + (timerHours * 60 * 60 * 1000);
        distance = endTime - now;
      }
      let h = Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) / (1000 * 60 * 60));
      let m = Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60 * 60)) / (1000 * 60));
      let s = Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60)) / 1000);
      
      if(hrEl) hrEl.innerText = (h < 10 ? '0' : '') + h;
      if(minEl) minEl.innerText = (m < 10 ? '0' : '') + m;
      if(secEl) secEl.innerText = (s < 10 ? '0' : '') + s;
    }
    updateTimer(); 
    setInterval(updateTimer, 1000);

    const track = document.getElementById('luxeTrack-' + sectionId);
    if (!track) return;
    const slides = Array.from(track.querySelectorAll('.luxe-slide'));
    const dots = document.querySelectorAll('#luxeDots-' + sectionId + ' .luxe-dot');
    const textArea = document.getElementById('luxeTextArea-' + sectionId);
    const titleEl = document.getElementById('luxeTitle-' + sectionId);
    const priceEl = document.getElementById('luxePrice-' + sectionId);
    const compareEl = document.getElementById('luxeCompare-' + sectionId);
    const btnEl = document.getElementById('luxeBtn-' + sectionId);
    
    const speed = parseInt(wrapper.getAttribute('data-speed')) || 4000;
    let currentIndex = 0;
    let autoPlayInterval = null;
    let swipeDirection = 1;
    let isPriceUnlocked = false; 
    let isHolding = false;
    let holdInterval = null;
    let holdTimeout = null;
    let holdPercent = 0;
    
    const btnProgress = btnEl.querySelector('.luxe-btn-progress');
    const btnText = btnEl.querySelector('.luxe-btn-text');

    function startHold(e) {
      if (isPriceUnlocked || isHolding) return;
      isHolding = true; holdPercent = 0;
      holdInterval = setInterval(() => {
        if (holdPercent < 100) { holdPercent += 5; if(btnProgress) btnProgress.style.width = holdPercent + '%'; }
      }, 50);
      holdTimeout = setTimeout(triggerUnlock, 1000);
    }

    function cancelHold() {
      if (isPriceUnlocked || !isHolding) return;
      isHolding = false; clearTimeout(holdTimeout); clearInterval(holdInterval);
      if (btnProgress) { btnProgress.style.width = '0%'; }
    }

    function triggerUnlock() {
      isHolding = false; clearTimeout(holdTimeout); clearInterval(holdInterval);
      isPriceUnlocked = true; stopAutoPlay();
      if (btnProgress) btnProgress.style.width = '100%';
      const activeSlide = slides[currentIndex];
      
      let priceTxt = activeSlide.getAttribute('data-price');
      if (!priceTxt.includes('Rs.') && !priceTxt.includes('$')) priceTxt = 'Rs. ' + priceTxt;
      
      priceEl.innerHTML = '<span class="luxe-unlocked-text">' + priceTxt + '</span>';
      
      const compVal = activeSlide.getAttribute('data-compare');
      if (compVal && compVal.trim() !== '') {
        let compTxt = compVal;
        if (!compTxt.includes('Rs.') && !compTxt.includes('$')) compTxt = 'Rs. ' + compTxt;
        compareEl.innerText = compTxt;
        compareEl.classList.add('is-visible');
      }

      setTimeout(() => {
        btnEl.classList.remove('is-locked');
        btnEl.classList.add('is-unlocked');
        if (btnText) btnText.innerText = 'Add To Bag';
        btnEl.href = activeSlide.getAttribute('data-url');
        if (btnProgress) btnProgress.style.display = 'none';
      }, 300);
    }

    btnEl.addEventListener('mousedown', startHold);
    btnEl.addEventListener('touchstart', startHold, {passive: true});
    window.addEventListener('mouseup', cancelHold);
    window.addEventListener('touchend', cancelHold);
    btnEl.addEventListener('mouseleave', cancelHold);
    btnEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (!isPriceUnlocked) e.preventDefault(); });

    // Ensure links inside slider don't trigger click on drag
    let startX = 0, startY = 0;
    track.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => { startX = e.clientX; startY = e.clientY; });
    track.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (Math.abs(e.clientX - startX) > 5 || Math.abs(e.clientY - startY) > 5) e.preventDefault();
    });

    function updateTextForSlide(index) {
      if (!slides[index]) return;
      textArea.style.opacity = '0';
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        const activeSlide = slides[index];
        titleEl.innerText = activeSlide.getAttribute('data-title') || '';
        isPriceUnlocked = true;
        
        let priceTxt = activeSlide.getAttribute('data-price');
        if (!priceTxt.includes('Rs.') && !priceTxt.includes('$')) priceTxt = 'Rs. ' + priceTxt;
        priceEl.innerText = priceTxt;
        
        const compVal = activeSlide.getAttribute('data-compare');
        if (compVal && compVal.trim() !== '') {
          let compTxt = compVal;
          if (!compTxt.includes('Rs.') && !compTxt.includes('$')) compTxt = 'Rs. ' + compTxt;
          compareEl.innerText = compTxt;
          compareEl.classList.add('is-visible');
        } else {
          compareEl.classList.remove('is-visible');
        }
        
        btnEl.classList.remove('is-locked');
        btnEl.classList.add('is-unlocked');
        if (btnText) btnText.innerText = 'View Deal';
        if (btnProgress) btnProgress.style.display = 'none';
        btnEl.href = activeSlide.getAttribute('data-url');
        
        textArea.style.opacity = '1';
      }, 300);
    }

    const slideObserver = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
      entries.forEach(entry => {
        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
          const index = slides.indexOf(entry.target);
          if (index !== -1 && index !== currentIndex) {
            currentIndex = index;
            dots.forEach(d => d.classList.remove('is-active'));
            if(dots[currentIndex]) dots[currentIndex].classList.add('is-active');
            updateTextForSlide(currentIndex);
          }
        }
      });
    }, { root: track, threshold: 0.5 });
    
    slides.forEach(slide => slideObserver.observe(slide));
    updateTextForSlide(0);

    function scrollToSlide(index) {
      if (!slides[index]) return;
      track.scrollTo({ left: slides[index].offsetLeft, behavior: 'smooth' });
    }

    function autoScroll() {
      if (slides.length <= 1) return;
      if (currentIndex >= slides.length - 1) swipeDirection = -1;
      else if (currentIndex <= 0) swipeDirection = 1;
      scrollToSlide(currentIndex + swipeDirection);
    }

    function startAutoPlay() { if (slides.length > 1) autoPlayInterval = setInterval(autoScroll, speed); }
    function stopAutoPlay() { clearInterval(autoPlayInterval); }

    dots.forEach((dot) => {
      dot.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        stopAutoPlay();
        scrollToSlide(parseInt(e.currentTarget.getAttribute('data-index')));
        if(!isPriceUnlocked) startAutoPlay();
      });
    });

    startAutoPlay();
  }

  function initAll() { document.querySelectorAll('.luxe-wrapper').forEach(initLuxeDeal); }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') { document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initAll); } else { initAll(); }
  if (window.Shopify && window.Shopify.designMode) {
    document.addEventListener('shopify:section:load', (e) => {
      const wrapper = e.target.querySelector('.luxe-wrapper');
      if (wrapper) initLuxeDeal(wrapper);
    });
  }
})();
</script></div><div id="shopify-section-template--29393177444433__11fit_2x2_grid_F4nxF3" class="shopify-section">

<script>
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    
    // --- CAROUSEL AUTO-SWIPE & DOTS SYNC LOGIC ---
    const track = document.getElementById('11fitTrendTrack-template--29393177444433__11fit_flash_sale_L4kxCW');
    const dotsWrap = document.getElementById('11fitTrendDots-template--29393177444433__11fit_flash_sale_L4kxCW');
    const wrapper = document.getElementById('11fitTrendWrapper-template--29393177444433__11fit_flash_sale_L4kxCW');
    
    if (!track) return;

    const dots = dotsWrap ? dotsWrap.querySelectorAll('.fit11-dot') : [];
    const totalItems = dots.length;
    let currentIndex = 0;
    
    const speed = parseInt(wrapper.getAttribute('data-speed')) || 3000;
    let autoPlayInterval;

    // 1. Sync dots when user manually scrolls
    track.addEventListener('scroll', function() {
      if(totalItems === 0) return;
      const scrollPos = track.scrollLeft;
      const cardWidth = track.clientWidth;
      const newIndex = Math.round(scrollPos / cardWidth);

      if (newIndex !== currentIndex && newIndex >= 0 && newIndex < totalItems) {
        dots[currentIndex].classList.remove('active');
        currentIndex = newIndex;
        dots[currentIndex].classList.add('active');
      }
    });

    // 2. Click a dot to jump to that product
    if(totalItems > 0) {
      dots.forEach((dot, i) => {
        dot.addEventListener('click', function() {
          const cardWidth = track.clientWidth;
          track.scrollTo({ left: cardWidth * i, behavior: 'smooth' });
        });
      });
    }

    // 3. Auto-swipe function
    function scrollToNextItem() {
      const cardWidth = track.clientWidth;
      const maxScroll = track.scrollWidth - track.clientWidth;

      // Reset to beginning if at the end
      if (track.scrollLeft >= maxScroll - 10) {
        track.scrollTo({ left: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
      } else {
        track.scrollBy({ left: cardWidth, behavior: 'smooth' });
      }
    }

    function startAutoPlay() {
      if(totalItems > 1) {
        autoPlayInterval = setInterval(scrollToNextItem, speed);
      }
    }

    function stopAutoPlay() {
      clearInterval(autoPlayInterval);
    }

    // 4. Pause swiping when user interacts
    wrapper.addEventListener('touchstart', stopAutoPlay, {passive: true});
    wrapper.addEventListener('touchend', startAutoPlay, {passive: true});
    wrapper.addEventListener('mouseenter', stopAutoPlay);
    wrapper.addEventListener('mouseleave', startAutoPlay);

    // Start immediately
    startAutoPlay();
  });
</script>

<script>
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const wrapper = document.getElementById('11fitTabsWrapper-template--29393177444433__11fit_tabbed_slider_wJVpcN');
    if (!wrapper) return;

    const navTrack = document.getElementById('11fitTabsNav-template--29393177444433__11fit_tabbed_slider_wJVpcN');
    const tabButtons = wrapper.querySelectorAll('.fit11-tab-btn');
    const tabPanels = wrapper.querySelectorAll('.fit11-tab-panel');
    
    // Auto-swipe settings
    const speed = parseInt(wrapper.getAttribute('data-speed')) || 3000;
    let autoPlayInterval;
    
    let navDirection = 1; 
    let prodDirections = {}; // Keeps track of swipe direction for each individual tab panel

    // --- MANUAL TAB CLICKING LOGIC ---
    tabButtons.forEach(button => {
      button.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
        tabButtons.forEach(btn => btn.classList.remove('active'));
        tabPanels.forEach(panel => panel.classList.remove('active'));

        this.classList.add('active');
        const targetPanel = document.getElementById(this.getAttribute('data-target'));
        if (targetPanel) {
          targetPanel.classList.add('active');
        }
        
        // Center the clicked tab smoothly without scrolling the entire page vertically
        if (navTrack) {
          const scrollLeft = this.offsetLeft - (navTrack.clientWidth / 2) + (this.clientWidth / 2);
          navTrack.scrollTo({ left: scrollLeft, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }
      });
    });

    // --- AUTO-SWIPE LOGIC ---
    function performAutoSwipes() {
      const activeSlider = wrapper.querySelector('.fit11-tab-panel.active .fit11-tab-slider');
      let switchedTab = false;

      if (activeSlider && activeSlider.scrollWidth > activeSlider.clientWidth) {
        const maxProdScroll = activeSlider.scrollWidth - activeSlider.clientWidth;
        const card = activeSlider.querySelector('.fit11-tab-card');
        const scrollAmount = card ? card.offsetWidth + 16 : 256;

        if (activeSlider.scrollLeft >= maxProdScroll - 10) {
          // Reached the end of products, switch to next tab
          switchedTab = true;
        } else {
          // Keep scrolling products
          activeSlider.scrollBy({ left: scrollAmount, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }
      } else {
        // No products to scroll (or they all fit on screen), just switch tab
        switchedTab = true;
      }

      if (switchedTab) {
        const activeBtn = wrapper.querySelector('.fit11-tab-btn.active');
        if (activeBtn) {
          let nextBtn = activeBtn.nextElementSibling;
          if (!nextBtn || !nextBtn.classList.contains('fit11-tab-btn')) {
            nextBtn = tabButtons[0]; // Loop back to start
          }
          nextBtn.click(); // Triggers the existing manual click logic

          // Ensure the new active slider starts at the very left
          setTimeout(() => {
            const newActiveSlider = wrapper.querySelector('.fit11-tab-panel.active .fit11-tab-slider');
            if (newActiveSlider) {
              newActiveSlider.scrollTo({left: 0, behavior: 'instant'});
            }
          }, 50);
        }
      }
    }

    function startAutoPlay() {
      autoPlayInterval = setInterval(performAutoSwipes, speed);
    }

    function stopAutoPlay() {
      clearInterval(autoPlayInterval);
    }

    // Pause everything if the user touches or hovers over the section
    wrapper.addEventListener('touchstart', stopAutoPlay, {passive: true});
    wrapper.addEventListener('touchend', startAutoPlay, {passive: true});
    wrapper.addEventListener('mouseenter', stopAutoPlay);
    wrapper.addEventListener('mouseleave', startAutoPlay);

    // Start auto-play globally (it detects automatically if scrolling is needed)
    startAutoPlay();
  });
</script>

<script>
(function() {
  var sectionId = "template--29393177444433__11fit_club_reviews_dzNCHU";
  function initReviews() {
    var scrollEl = document.getElementById('fitReviewScroll-' + sectionId);
    var sectionEl = document.getElementById('fit-reviews-' + sectionId);
    if (!scrollEl || !sectionEl) return;

    var prevBtn = sectionEl.querySelector('.fit-review-nav.prev');
    var nextBtn = sectionEl.querySelector('.fit-review-nav.next');

    var revealObs = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
      entries.forEach(function(e) {
        if (e.isIntersecting) { e.target.classList.add('active'); revealObs.unobserve(e.target); }
      });
    }, { threshold: 0.1 });
    sectionEl.querySelectorAll('.fit-r-reveal').forEach(function(el) { revealObs.observe(el); });

    function getCardWidth() {
      var card = scrollEl.querySelector('.fit-review-card');
      if (!card) return 320;
      var track = scrollEl.querySelector('.fit-reviews-track');
      var gap = track ? (parseInt(window.getComputedStyle(track).gap) || 20) : 20;
      return card.offsetWidth + gap;
    }

    function moveSlider(dir) {
      var cardW = getCardWidth();
      var maxScroll = Math.floor(scrollEl.scrollWidth - scrollEl.clientWidth);
      var currentLeft = Math.ceil(scrollEl.scrollLeft);
      
      if (dir === 'next') {
        if (currentLeft >= maxScroll - 10) {
          scrollEl.scrollTo({ left: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
        } else {
          scrollEl.scrollBy({ left: cardW, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }
      } else {
        if (currentLeft <= 10) {
          scrollEl.scrollTo({ left: maxScroll, behavior: 'smooth' });
        } else {
          scrollEl.scrollBy({ left: -cardW, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }
      }
    }

    var autoInterval = null;
    var autoDelay = 3500;
    function startAuto() { if (autoDelay > 0) autoInterval = setInterval(function() { moveSlider('next'); }, autoDelay); }
    function stopAuto() { clearInterval(autoInterval); }
    function resetAuto() { stopAuto(); startAuto(); }

    startAuto();
    if (nextBtn) nextBtn.addEventListener('click', function() { moveSlider('next'); resetAuto(); });
    if (prevBtn) prevBtn.addEventListener('click', function() { moveSlider('prev'); resetAuto(); });
    scrollEl.addEventListener('touchstart', stopAuto, { passive: true });
    scrollEl.addEventListener('touchend', function() { setTimeout(startAuto, 2500); });
    scrollEl.addEventListener('mouseleave', startAuto);
  }

  // Run initialization immediately since script is at the bottom
  initReviews();

  // Support for Shopify theme editor events
  if (window.Shopify && Shopify.designMode) {
    document.addEventListener('shopify:section:load', function(event) {
      if (event.detail.sectionId === sectionId) {
        initReviews();
      }
    });
  }
})();
</script>

<script>
(function() {
  var sectionId = "template--29393177444433__11fit_support_faq_BAdFVN";
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
    var sectionEl = document.getElementById('fit-faq-' + sectionId);
    if (!sectionEl) return;

    var revealObs = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
      entries.forEach(function(e) {
        if (e.isIntersecting) { e.target.classList.add('active'); revealObs.unobserve(e.target); }
      });
    }, { threshold: 0.08 });
    sectionEl.querySelectorAll('.fit-faq-reveal').forEach(function(el) { revealObs.observe(el); });

    var items = sectionEl.querySelectorAll('.fit-faq-item');
    items.forEach(function(item) {
      item.querySelector('.fit-faq-question').addEventListener('click', function() {
        var isActive = item.classList.contains('active');
        items.forEach(function(el) { el.classList.remove('active'); });
        if (!isActive) item.classList.add('active');
      });
    });
  });
})();
</script>

</section>
    </main>

          const el = document.querySelector(pageContainers[i]);
          if (el) return el;
        }
        return null;
      };

      // Find the container and (on the tracking page) inject the widget mount point.
      // Returns true once there is nothing left to do, false to keep retrying.
      const ensureContainer = function() {
        const pageContainer = findContainer();
        if (pageContainer) {
          window.AVADA_ORDER_TRACKING.pageContainer = pageContainer;
        }
        if (!isTrackingPage) return true;
        if (document.querySelector('.Avada-OrderTracking__Wrapper')) return true;

      };

      // append (do NOT innerHTML-wipe — these are broad ancestors holding header/footer)

      const fallbackHosts = ['main', '[role="main"]', '#MainContent', '#main', 'body'];
      const mountFallback = function() {
        if (!isTrackingPage) return;
        if (document.querySelector('.Avada-OrderTracking__Wrapper')) return;
        let host = null;
        for (let i = 0; i < fallbackHosts.length; i++) {
          host = document.querySelector(fallbackHosts[i]);
          if (host) break;
        }
        if (!host) return;
        const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
        wrapper.className = 'Avada-OrderTracking__Wrapper';
        host.appendChild(wrapper);
        window.AVADA_ORDER_TRACKING.pageContainer = host;
      };

      // The embed runs synchronously and may execute before the theme has rendered
      // the page container into the DOM (or while it renders sections async), which
      // would leave pageContainer null forever. Retry on DOM ready + bounded polling,
      // with a MutationObserver fallback, until the container shows up; if it never
      // does (theme matches no selector), fall back to a guaranteed mount point.
      if (!ensureContainer()) {
        let attempts = 0;
        const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 40; // ~10s at 250ms
        let observer = null;
        const stop = function() {
          if (observer) { observer.disconnect(); observer = null; }
        };
        const retry = function() {
          if (ensureContainer()) { stop(); return; }
          attempts += 1;
          if (attempts >= MAX_ATTEMPTS) { mountFallback(); stop(); return; }
          setTimeout(retry, 250);
        };
        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
          document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', retry, {once: true});
        }
        if (window.MutationObserver) {
          observer = new MutationObserver(function() {
            if (ensureContainer()) stop();
          });
          observer.observe(document.documentElement, {childList: true, subtree: true});
        }
        setTimeout(retry, 250);
      }
    }
  } catch(e) {

<script>
  function removeFit11Preloader() {
    var p = document.getElementById('fit11-preloader');
    if (p && p.style.visibility !== 'hidden') {
      p.style.opacity = '0';
      p.style.visibility = 'hidden';
      document.documentElement.classList.add('page-loaded');
    }
    sessionStorage.setItem('fit11_loaded', 'true');
  }
  
  function addVariantToCart(variantId, button) {
    if (!variantId) return;
    if (button) {
      button.classList.add('loading');
      button.disabled = true;
    }

    const cart = document.querySelector('cart-notification') || document.querySelector('cart-drawer');
    const sections = cart ? cart.getSectionsToRender().map((s) => s.id) : [];

    const body = JSON.stringify({
      id: variantId,
      quantity: 1,
      sections: sections,
      sections_url: window.location.pathname
    });

    fetch('/cart/add.js', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest'
      },
      body: body
    })
    .then(response => response.json())
    .then(parsedState => {
      if (parsedState.status) {
        alert(parsedState.description || parsedState.message);
        return;
      }
      if (cart) {
        if (cart.classList.contains('is-empty')) cart.classList.remove('is-empty');
        cart.renderContents(parsedState);
      } else {
        window.location = '/cart';
      }
    })
    .catch(error => {
      console.error('Error adding variant to cart:', error);
    })
    .finally(() => {
      if (button) {
        button.classList.remove('loading');
        button.disabled = false;
      }
    });
  }

  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() { 
    setTimeout(removeFit11Preloader, 600); 

    // Initialize Global Scroll Observer
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
      entries.forEach(entry => {
        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
          entry.target.classList.add('is-visible', 'scroll-trigger--animate');
          observer.unobserve(entry.target); // Only animate once
        }
      });
    }, { rootMargin: "0px 0px -50px 0px", threshold: 0.1 });

    document.querySelectorAll('.pc-reveal, .pc-reveal-stagger, .scroll-trigger').forEach(el => observer.observe(el));

    // Autoplay Product Card Sliders (Optimized to prevent layout thrashing)
    setInterval(() => {
      const sliders = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.fit-card-slider'));
      const scrollOps = [];
      // Phase 1: Read all DOM properties to avoid layout thrashing
      sliders.forEach(slider => {
        if (!slider.isHovered && slider.scrollWidth > slider.clientWidth) {
          scrollOps.push({
            el: slider,
            width: slider.clientWidth,
            reset: slider.scrollLeft + slider.clientWidth >= slider.scrollWidth - 10
          });
        }
      });
      // Phase 2: Perform all DOM writes
      scrollOps.forEach(op => {
        if (op.reset) {
          op.el.scrollTo({ left: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
        } else {
          op.el.scrollBy({ left: op.width, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }
      });
    }, 3000);

    document.querySelectorAll('.fit-card-slider').forEach(slider => {
      slider.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => slider.isHovered = true);
      slider.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => slider.isHovered = false);
      slider.addEventListener('touchstart', () => slider.isHovered = true, {passive: true});
      slider.addEventListener('touchend', () => { setTimeout(() => slider.isHovered = false, 3000); }, {passive: true});
    });

    // Color Swatch Clicks
    document.querySelectorAll('.fit-color-dot').forEach(dot => {
      dot.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const slideIndex = parseInt(dot.getAttribute('data-slide-index') || '0', 10);
        const cardMedia = dot.closest('.card__media');
        const slider = cardMedia ? cardMedia.querySelector('.fit-card-slider') : null;
        if (slider) {
          slider.scrollTo({ left: slideIndex * slider.clientWidth, behavior: 'smooth' });
          // Temporarily pause autoplay
          slider.isHovered = true;
          setTimeout(() => slider.isHovered = false, 5000);
        }
      });
    });
  });
  setTimeout(removeFit11Preloader, 2000);
  window.addEventListener('load', function() { setTimeout(removeFit11Preloader, 400); });
</script>

<div id="lx-variant-modal" style="display:none; position:fixed; top:0; left:0; right:0; bottom:0; background:rgba(0,0,0,0.5); z-index:999999; align-items:flex-end; justify-content:center;" onclick="if(event.target===this)lxCloseVariantModal()">
  <div style="background:#fff; border-radius:16px 16px 0 0; padding:24px 20px; width:100%; max-width:480px; max-height:80vh; overflow-y:auto; position:relative; font-family:'Outfit', sans-serif; box-sizing:border-box;">

        <p id="lx-vm-price" style="font-weight:600;font-size:13px;color:#B89356;margin:0;"></p>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div id="lx-vm-options"></div>
    <button type="button" id="lx-vm-add-btn" onclick="lxAddSelectedVariant()" style="width:100%;background:#1A202C;color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:10px;padding:14px;font-size:15px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;margin-top:16px;font-family:inherit;">Add to Cart</button>
  </div>

<script>
(function() {
  var _vmProduct = null;
  var _vmSelectedOptions = {};
  var _vmLineIndex = null;
  var _vmQty = null;

  window.lxOpenVariantModal = function(productId) {
    var p = window._lxProducts && window._lxProducts[productId];
    if (!p) return;
    _vmProduct = p;
    _vmSelectedOptions = {};
    _vmLineIndex = null;
    _vmQty = null;

    // featured_image can be an object {src:...} or a plain URL string
    var imgSrc = '';
    if (p.featured_image) {
      imgSrc = typeof p.featured_image === 'object' ? (p.featured_image.src || '') : p.featured_image;
    }
    document.getElementById('lx-vm-img').src = imgSrc;
    document.getElementById('lx-vm-title').textContent = p.title;
    document.getElementById('lx-vm-price').textContent = (p.price / 100).toLocaleString('en-IN', { style: 'currency', currency: 'INR', minimumFractionDigits: 0 });

    var addBtn = document.getElementById('lx-vm-add-btn');
    addBtn.textContent = 'Add to Cart';
    addBtn.style.background = '#1A202C';
    addBtn.disabled = false;

    lxRenderOptions(p);
    document.getElementById('lx-variant-modal').style.display = 'flex';
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
  };

  window.lxOpenChangeVariant = function(productId, lineIndex, qty) {
    fetch(window.Shopify.routes.root + 'products/' + productId + '.js')
      .then(function(r){ return r.json(); })
      .then(function(p) {
        _vmProduct = p;
        _vmSelectedOptions = {};
        _vmLineIndex = lineIndex;
        _vmQty = qty;

        document.getElementById('lx-vm-img').src = p.featured_image ? p.featured_image : '';
        document.getElementById('lx-vm-title').textContent = p.title;
        document.getElementById('lx-vm-price').textContent = (p.price / 100).toLocaleString('en-IN', { style: 'currency', currency: 'INR', minimumFractionDigits: 0 });

        var addBtn = document.getElementById('lx-vm-add-btn');
        addBtn.textContent = 'Update Variant';
        addBtn.style.background = '#B89356';
        addBtn.disabled = false;

        lxRenderOptions(p);
        document.getElementById('lx-variant-modal').style.display = 'flex';
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      });
  };
  function lxRenderOptions(p) {
    var optHtml = '';
    p.options.forEach(function(opt, oi) {
      // Recommendations API returns opt as an object {name: 'Color', values: [...]}, while other endpoints might return strings
      var optName = typeof opt === 'object' && opt.name ? opt.name : opt;
      
      // Recommendations API variants use option1, option2, option3 instead of an options[] array
      var vals = p.variants.map(function(v) { 
        return v.options ? v.options[oi] : v['option' + (oi + 1)]; 
      }).filter(function(v,i,a){ return v != null && a.indexOf(v)===i; });

      optHtml += '<div style="margin-bottom:14px;">';
      optHtml += '<p style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.5px;color:#6B7280;margin:0 0 8px;">' + optName + '</p>';
      optHtml += '<div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;">';
      vals.forEach(function(val) {
        optHtml += '<button type="button" class="lx-vm-opt-btn" data-option-index="' + oi + '" data-value="' + val + '" onclick="lxSelectOpt(this,' + oi + ',\'' + val.replace(/'/g,"\\'") + '\')" style="border:1.5px solid #E5E7EB;border-radius:6px;padding:6px 12px;font-size:13px;font-weight:500;background:#fff;cursor:pointer;transition:all 0.2s;">' + val + '</button>';
      });
      optHtml += '</div></div>';
    });
    document.getElementById('lx-vm-options').innerHTML = optHtml;
  }

  window.lxCloseVariantModal = function() {
    document.getElementById('lx-variant-modal').style.display = 'none';
    document.body.style.overflow = '';
    _vmProduct = null;
    _vmSelectedOptions = {};
    _vmLineIndex = null;
    _vmQty = null;
  };

  window.lxSelectOpt = function(btn, optIndex, val) {
    var btns = document.querySelectorAll('.lx-vm-opt-btn[data-option-index="' + optIndex + '"]');
    btns.forEach(function(b) { b.style.borderColor='#E5E7EB'; b.style.background='#fff'; b.style.color='#1A202C'; b.style.fontWeight='500'; });
    btn.style.borderColor = '#1A202C';
    btn.style.background = '#1A202C';
    btn.style.color = '#fff';
    btn.style.fontWeight = '700';
    _vmSelectedOptions[optIndex] = val;
  };

  window.lxAddSelectedVariant = function() {
    if (!_vmProduct) return;
    var numOpts = _vmProduct.options ? _vmProduct.options.length : 0;
    if (Object.keys(_vmSelectedOptions).length < numOpts) {
      var addBtn = document.getElementById('lx-vm-add-btn');
      var orig = addBtn.textContent;
      addBtn.textContent = 'Please select all options';
      addBtn.style.background = '#DC2626';
      setTimeout(function() { addBtn.textContent = orig; addBtn.style.background = _vmLineIndex ? '#B89356' : '#1A202C'; }, 1500);
      return;
    }
    var matched = _vmProduct.variants.find(function(v) {
      return v.options.every(function(o, i) { return _vmSelectedOptions[i] === o; });
    });
    if (!matched) return;
    var addBtn = document.getElementById('lx-vm-add-btn');
    addBtn.disabled = true;

    if (_vmLineIndex) {
      addBtn.textContent = 'Updating...';
      fetch(window.Shopify.routes.root + 'cart/change.js', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ line: _vmLineIndex, quantity: 0 })
      }).then(function() {
        return fetch(window.Shopify.routes.root + 'cart/add.js', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ id: matched.id, quantity: _vmQty || 1 })
        });
      }).then(function() {
        addBtn.textContent = '✓ Updated!';
        addBtn.style.background = '#16A34A';
        setTimeout(function() { lxCloseVariantModal(); location.reload(); }, 700);
      }).catch(function() {
        addBtn.textContent = 'Update Variant';
        addBtn.disabled = false;
      });
    } else {
      addBtn.textContent = 'Adding...';
      fetch(window.Shopify.routes.root + 'cart/add.js', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id: matched.id, quantity: 1 })
      }).then(function() {
        addBtn.textContent = '✓ Added!';
        addBtn.style.background = '#16A34A';
        setTimeout(function() { lxCloseVariantModal(); location.reload(); }, 700);
      }).catch(function() {
        addBtn.textContent = 'Add to Cart';
        addBtn.disabled = false;
      });
    }
  };

  window.lxDirectAdd = function(btn, variantId) {
    btn.textContent = 'Adding...';
    btn.disabled = true;
    fetch(window.Shopify.routes.root + 'cart/add.js', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ id: variantId, quantity: 1 })
    }).then(function() {
      btn.textContent = '✓ Added!';
      btn.style.background = '#16A34A';
      setTimeout(function() { location.reload(); }, 800);
    }).catch(function() {
      btn.textContent = '+ Quick Add';
      btn.disabled = false;
    });
  };

  // Remove a single cart line item by its unique item key
  window.lxRemoveCartItem = function(itemKey, btn) {
    btn.textContent = '...';
    btn.disabled = true;
    var item = btn.closest('.lx-cart-item');
    var updatesObj = {};
    updatesObj[itemKey] = 0;
    fetch(window.Shopify.routes.root + 'cart/update.js', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ updates: updatesObj })
    })
    .then(function(res) { return res.json(); })
    .then(function() {
      location.reload();
    })
    .catch(function() {
      btn.textContent = 'Remove';
      btn.disabled = false;
    });
  };

  window.lxDirectChangeVariant = function(selectEl) {
    var picker = selectEl.closest('.lx-direct-picker');
    var itemKey = picker.getAttribute('data-item-key');
    var qty = parseInt(picker.getAttribute('data-qty'), 10);
    var selects = picker.querySelectorAll('.lx-direct-option-select');
    
    var selectedOptions = [];
    selects.forEach(function(sel) {
      var optIdx = parseInt(sel.getAttribute('data-option-index'), 10);
      selectedOptions[optIdx] = sel.value;
    });
    
    var jsonEl = picker.querySelector('.lx-direct-variants-json');
    if (!jsonEl) return;
    var variants = JSON.parse(jsonEl.textContent);
    
    var matched = variants.find(function(v) {
      return v.options.every(function(optVal, idx) {
        return selectedOptions[idx] === optVal;
      });
    });
    
    if (!matched) {
      alert('This combination is not available.');
      return;
    }
    
    selects.forEach(function(sel) { sel.disabled = true; });
    
    fetch(window.Shopify.routes.root + 'cart/change.js', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ id: itemKey, quantity: 0 })
    }).then(function() {
      return fetch(window.Shopify.routes.root + 'cart/add.js', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id: matched.id, quantity: qty })
      });
    }).then(function() {
      location.reload();
    }).catch(function() {
      selects.forEach(function(sel) { sel.disabled = false; });
    });
  };
  // --- GLOBAL VIP AUTO-APPLY & AUTO-REMOVE SYSTEM ---
  let isVerifyingVip = false;

  async function verifyVipState(isInitialLoad) {
    if (isVerifyingVip) return;
    
    const config = window.lxVipConfig || { vipCode: 'PURCHASE1999', vipThreshold: 250000 };
    const vipCode = config.vipCode;
    const vipThreshold = config.vipThreshold;
    
    if (!vipCode || vipCode.trim() === '') return;

    isVerifyingVip = true;
    const attemptKey = 'lx_vip_attempt_' + vipCode.toUpperCase();

    try {
      const cartRes = await fetch(window.Shopify.routes.root + 'cart.js');
      const cart = await cartRes.json();
      
      const cartTotal = cart.total_price;
      const appliedDiscounts = cart.cart_level_discount_applications.map(d => d.title.toUpperCase());
      const hasVipCode = appliedDiscounts.includes(vipCode.toUpperCase());
      const manualCode = localStorage.getItem('lx_coupon_code');
      
      // LOGIC 1: Cart is above threshold, but code is NOT applied -> Try to apply it.
      if (cartTotal >= vipThreshold && !hasVipCode) {
        const attemptStatus = sessionStorage.getItem(attemptKey);
        if (attemptStatus === 'trying') {
          sessionStorage.setItem(attemptKey, 'failed');
          return;
        } else if (attemptStatus === 'failed') {
          return;
        }

        sessionStorage.setItem(attemptKey, 'trying');

        let combinedCodes = vipCode;
        if (manualCode && manualCode.toUpperCase() !== vipCode.toUpperCase()) {
          combinedCodes += ',' + manualCode;
        }

        await fetch(window.Shopify.routes.root + 'cart/update.js', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ discount: combinedCodes })
        });
        
        if (isInitialLoad) {
          location.reload();
        } else {
          refreshCartUI();
        }
      } 
      // LOGIC 2: Successfully applied -> Clear flags
      else if (cartTotal >= vipThreshold && hasVipCode) {
        sessionStorage.removeItem(attemptKey);
        const errorNotice = document.getElementById('lx-vip-error-notice');
        if (errorNotice) errorNotice.style.display = 'none';
      }
      // LOGIC 3: Cart dropped below threshold, but VIP is still applied -> Remove it.
      else if (cartTotal < vipThreshold && hasVipCode) {
        await fetch(window.Shopify.routes.root + 'cart/update.js', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ discount: manualCode || '' }) 
        });
        sessionStorage.removeItem(attemptKey);
        
        if (isInitialLoad) {
          location.reload();
        } else {
          refreshCartUI();
        }
      }
      // LOGIC 4: Cart is below threshold and it is correctly not applied -> Clear flags.
      else if (cartTotal < vipThreshold && !hasVipCode) {
         sessionStorage.removeItem(attemptKey);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('VIP Verification Error:', err);
    } finally {
      isVerifyingVip = false;
    }
  }

  function refreshCartUI() {
    const drawerItems = document.querySelector('cart-drawer-items');
    const cartItems = document.querySelector('cart-items');
    if (drawerItems) {
      drawerItems.onCartUpdate();
    }
    if (cartItems) {
      cartItems.onCartUpdate();
    }
  }

  // Check state on initial page load
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    verifyVipState(true);
  });

  // WA CART TRACKING PREP
  const WA_API_BASE = 'https://checkout-app-one-lilac.vercel.app/api';
  const MERCHANT_KEY = 'sk_live_11fit_106b31bb8dd7a7';
  let waDeviceId = localStorage.getItem('fit11_device_id');
  if (!waDeviceId) {
    waDeviceId = 'dev_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9) + Date.now();
    localStorage.setItem('fit11_device_id', waDeviceId);
  }


  async function trackCartSilently() {
    try {
      const cartRes = await fetch('/cart.js');
      const cart = await cartRes.json();
      
      const cartHash = cart.items ? cart.items.map(i => i.id + '-' + i.quantity).join('|') : '';
      const lastHash = sessionStorage.getItem('last_tracked_cart');
      if (lastHash === cartHash) return; // Do not send if exactly same
      sessionStorage.setItem('last_tracked_cart', cartHash);

      const utmData = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('wa_utm_data') || '{}');
      await fetch(`${WA_API_BASE}/checkout/track-cart`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ merchant_key: MERCHANT_KEY, device_id: waDeviceId, cart_details: { ...cart, utm_data: utmData } })
      });
    } catch(e) {}
  }

  // Intercept AJAX cart updates (when user changes quantity + / - or changes variants)
  const originalFetch = window.fetch;
  window.fetch = async function() {
    const response = await originalFetch.apply(this, arguments);
    const url = arguments[0];
    
    if (typeof url === 'string' && (url.includes('/cart/change') || url.includes('/cart/add') || url.includes('/cart/update'))) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        verifyVipState(false);
        trackCartSilently();
      }, 400); 
    }
    return response;
  };
  
  // Also track immediately on page load to catch any items added via non-fetch forms
  setTimeout(() => {
    trackCartSilently();
  }, 1500);

  window.lxRefreshCartUI = refreshCartUI;
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
